| Coins jingle, fabric twirls and music flows around Dancer Mice, the masters of movement. Dancer Mice make their trade look easy, but the tricks they pull off are anything but-- cartwheels, back flips and amazing feats of balance fill their routines. With so much strength and agility at their command, it’s no wonder that Dancer Mice can pirouette their way to the cheese. |  |  |  |

Dancer Mouse is only attracted to Gauntlet Cheese Tier 4.

Dancer Mouse can drop Gauntlet Potion Tier 5 as loot.

## History and Trivia

- 8 June 2010: Dancer Mouse was released as part of King's Gauntlet location.
- 29 June 2010: The King granted permission to use the Gauntlet Potion Tier 4, allowing Hunters to access the Dancer Mouse.

# 🐭 Dangerous Duo Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Dangerous Duo Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnian Express Station. It can only be attracted and caught during the Raider River phase, where they are known to drop Copper Beads as loot.

| Dangerous Duo Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Train Robbers (Raider) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 11,000 | Gold: | 14,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Law | Other Requirements: | Raider River Stage |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | Roof Rack CharmorDoor Guard CharmorGreasy Glob Charm |
| Locations: | Gnawnian Express Station | Loot: | Copper Bead |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Inseparable since the day they could walk, this dangerous duo has been stealing cheese from traps since they were very young. With one always watching over the other, it's nigh impossible to get the drop on these two!<br>Their natural skill and experience in the thieving world landed them a job robbing trains with the raiders. Though they work extremely well together, they aren't much when not together. They must be caught as a pair in order to fend off the raiders.<br>"Two tails are better than one!" --Famous Dangerous Duo catchphrase.<br>Catching this mouse will decrease the number of Raiders Left by two but is only encountered when using the correct charms. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 507 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Dangerous Duo mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against the Dangerous Duo Mouse. They are only attracted when the correct charm (Roof Rack, Door Guard, or Greasy Glob) for the current wave is armed, and do not visit during Trap Checks.

## Event Participation

- The Dangerous Duo Mouse drops the Gnawnian Express Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 26 September 2013 as part of the Gnawnian Express Station content.

# 🐭 Dark Magi Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Dark Magi Mouse is a breed of mouse that can be found in the Cursed City. It is known to drop Ancient Relic, Dark Chest, and Living Garden Theme Scrap 2 as loot.

| Dark Magi Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Lost City Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 195,000 | Gold: | 24,500 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Arcane | Other Requirements: | See Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame |
| Cheese: | Graveblossom Camembert | Charm: | Shattering Charm |
| Locations: | Cursed City | Loot: | Ancient RelicDark ChestLiving Garden Theme Scrap 2 |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This mouse is said to have been one of the first to walk these lands. Shrouded in darkness, he whispers cursed promises to those weak of heart. Anyone foolish enough to strike a deal with this sinister foe will surely find themselves forever lost with their wishes twisted to his intent. This mouse has formidable powers not of this world, and anyone seeking to hunt him will have to steel their soul and prepare to live a cursed life. Everything to do with this mouse comes with a price... |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 452 |  |  |

## Hunting Strategy

Dark Magi Mouse can only be attracted with Graveblossom Camembert and Shattering Charm armed.

It is a member of the Lost City Mice group. As such all power types except Arcane weapons are ineffective against it.

It is only available in Curses Lifted state in the Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame.

## History and Trivia

- 14 January 2013: The Dark Magi Mouse was introduced as part of the Living Garden expansion.

# 🐭 Dashing Buccaneer Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Dashing Buccaneer Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Harbour.

| Dashing Buccaneer Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Dock Dwellers |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 375 | Gold: | 250 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Hydro | Other Requirements: | Pirate Bounty |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Harbour | Loot: | Marble CheeseSatchel of Gold (250 gold)Simple Orb |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| With a slash and a dash, these mice hit hard and fast! Not one to let a pegged leg slow them down, they are the forefront of every pirate attack and are the first aboard enemy ships!<br>It looks like this one has had a run in with a Travelling Barber Mouse or two... |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 862 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Dashing Buccaneer Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Hunters must have an active Pirate Bounty to attract this mouse.

Hunters must capture a Pirate Mouse to obtain a pirate bounty. When Hunters begin the search for the mice listed on the crew manifest provided by the Pirate Mouse, the 5 Pirate Crew mice are added to the mouse population of the Harbour. Capturing all 3 mice listed on the crew manifest will reward the player with booty from the pirate bounty.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Dashing Buccaneer Mouse drops the Pirate Crew Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- 11 April 2017: The Dashing Buccaneer Mouse was added to the game with the introduction of new content to the Harbour location.
- 17 October 2018: The Dashing Buccaneer Mouse changed mouse groups from Aquatic Order to Dock Dwellers.

|  | Mouse ID#: 1256 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Dawn Quixotic is only attracted to Storied Saga.

## Hunting Strategy

The Dawn Quixotic can only be attracted while writing a story.

Only Law power types are effective against Dawn Quixotic.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 13 January 2026: Dawn Quixotic was released as part of Conclusion Cliffs location.

# 🐭 Deep Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

> "Deep" redirects here. For more uses, see Deep (disambiguation).

The Deep Mouse is a breed of mouse found in The Deep Lair zone of the Iceberg. It is known to drop Ancient Relic, Deep Thought (25,000 points), Plankrun's Iceberg Notes, and Satchel of Gold (10,000 gold) as loot.

| Deep Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Icewing's Invasion (Misc) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 142,550 | Gold: | 24,525 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Hydro | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Iceberg | Loot: | Ancient RelicDeep Thought (25,000 points)Plankrun's Iceberg NotesSatchel of Gold (10,000 gold) |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Finally solving the question as to how the Iceberg is propelled, the Deep Mouse lives well below the Iceberg, dragging it relentlessly towards the shores of Gnawnia.Only the most dedicated hunters will ever be able to encounter this monster, let alone defeat it! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 394 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Deep Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The Deep Mouse can only be encountered in The Deep Lair. Hunters must arm the Deep Freeze Base, Iceberg Boiler Base, or Ultimate Iceberg Base when catching Icewing and then complete the Hidden Depths in prior to hunt #250 to enter The Deep Lair and encounter the Deep Mouse. The Deep Mouse is a guaranteed catch on hunt 250, but only if attracted to the Hunter's trap. Therefore, it is recommended that Hunters arm SUPER|brie+ for that final hunt.

The quantities of Deep Thought (25,000 points) and Satchel of Gold (10,000 gold) dropped as loot is dependent on how many hunts remain of the allowed 250 when the Hunter catches the Deep Mouse. A successful catch of the Deep Mouse will award 1 each of Deep Thought and Satchel of Gold, with each hunt remaining in the allotted 250 awarding an additional 1 each, up to a maximum of 10 each in total.

## History and Trivia

- 14 June 2012: The Deep Mouse was released.
- 29 August 2012: The Deep Mouse began dropping Ancient Relic as loot.

| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro or Law | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Standard Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | DojoHarbourKing's ArmsMeadowMountainTournament HallTown of DigbyTown of GnawniaTraining GroundsWindmill | Loot: | Packet of FlourSatchel of Gold (500)Simple Orb |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Similar in rarity and strength to the gold mouse, the diamond mouse offers a high points reward to the hunter who can catch it. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 53 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Diamond Mouse can be attracted using Standard Cheese.

In the Town of Gnawnia location, the Diamond Mouse can also be encountered when hunting with Gilded Cheese.
In the Windmill location, the Diamond Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Grilled Cheese.
In the Mountain location, the Diamond Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Chedd-Ore Cheese or Abominable Asiago Cheese.
In the Dojo location, the Diamond Mouse can also be encountered when hunting with Maki Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Diamond Mouse.

Hunters may arm the Prospector's Charm in the Mountain location to improve the attraction rate of the Diamond Mouse, Gold Mouse, and Silvertail Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | AllexceptDraconic, Parental and Rift | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Diamond Mouse in the following locations:

- Gnawnia:

> Meadow
Town of Gnawnia
Windmill
Harbour
Mountain

- Valour:

> King's Arms
Tournament Hall

- Burroughs:

> Town of Digby

- Furoma:

> Training Grounds
Dojo

## Loot

The Diamond Mouse drops the following loot:

- Gnawnia:

> Meadow - Satchel of Gold (500)
Windmill - Packet of Flour and Simple Orb
Harbour - Simple Orb
Mountain - Simple Orb

- Burroughs:

> Town of Digby - Simple Orb

- Furoma:

> Training Grounds - Simple Orb
Dojo - Simple Orb

The Diamond Mouse is not known to drop any loot in the King's Arms, Tournament Hall, or Town of Gnawnia locations.

## History and Trivia

- 25 January 2011: The Diamond Mouse began to drop Simple Orb as loot.
- 4 April 2012: The Diamond Mouse began dropping a bonus Diamond Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event.
- 25 June 2013: The Diamond Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Raw Diamond Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Diamond Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Diamond Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 2 September 2014: The Diamond Mouse began to drop Packet of Flour at the Windmill.
- 17 October 2018: The Diamond Mouse changed mouse groups from Indigenous Mice to Mountain Mice.
- 5 October 2021: The Gold for Diamond Mouse was increased as part of the Town of Gnawnia and Windmill gameplay updates.

- 9 March 2010: The Dojo Sensei was released as part of the MouseHunt Birthday II event.
- 8 June 2010: The Dojo Sensei was originally named the Dojo Sensei Mouse. Its name was changed with the release of MouseHunt Version 3.0.
- 25 January 2011: The Dojo Sensei began to drop Divine Orbs as loot.
- 29 August 2012: The Dojo Sensei began dropping Ancient Relics as loot.

# 🐭 Dragon Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

> "Dragon" redirects here. For other uses, see Dragon (disambiguation).

The Dragon Mouse is a breed of mouse found in Dracano. It is known to drop Dragon's Chest, Dragon Ember, and Ancient Relic, as loot.

| Dragon Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Draconic Brood |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 74,200 | Gold: | 39,940 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Draconic | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Inferno Havarti | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Dracano | Loot: | Ancient RelicDragon's ChestDragon Ember |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| High above the summit of Dracano circles the terrible Dragon mouse, scourge of the Tribal Isles. To this date, no trap has withstood the Dragon's fiery breath, and all attempts to hunt it have reduced the most advanced traps to smoking debris. Legend has it that the Acolyte Mice held the key to controlling the Dragon, but now that they are trapped in another realm, the Dragon mouse has expanded its territory. The Tribal Isles need a brave MouseHunter to confront this menace, before it sets its sights on Gnawnia! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 56 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Dragon Mouse is only attracted to Inferno Havarti.

## Hunting Strategy

Draconic is the only power type that is very effective against the Dragon Mouse. All other weapon types are ineffective.

Dragonbane Charm, Super Dragonbane Charm, Extreme Dragonbane Charm, and Ultimate Dragonbane Charm provide a power bonus against the Dragon Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 29 September 2009: The Dragon Mouse was introduced with the release of the Dracano.
- According to HitGrab developer Jacob Johnson, "The dragon mouse image was created back in May of 2008. The dragon was pretty much just a few sketches until I found the one that I wanted to do, once the dragon was created it stayed as is until it was finally released. We just tailored the story to fit since the original intention didn't work out in the end. Hence why it ended up taking so long to come out." [1]
- The description of the Dragon Mouse has been shortened since its release. The original description was:

> Seen from a great distance, soaring high above the summit of Dracano, the Dragon mouse is terrible to behold. The mice of the Elub, Nerg and Derr have a mutual respect for the Dragon, often traveling through the treacherous Jungle of Dread to make offerings of Inferno cheese to keep the Dragon mouse from ransacking their villages. Lore tells us that the Dragon does have a weakness, but no Trapsmith has succeeded in building a trap that is powerful enough to withstand the might of the Dragon mouse. Their attempts have all resulted in a smoldering pile of debris, as no trap has been capable of withstanding its fiery breath. It has been said that long ago, before their defeat, the Acolyte mice discovered the Dragon Mouse’s true weakness. If it were not for the efforts of Plankrun, the Acolyte mice would have succeeded in their plan to enslave the Dragon mouse as a soldier in their armies. With the Acolyte mice long locked away in another realm, the Dragon mouse, lacking any opposition, has been slowly expanding its territory. Now with its sights set across the Rodentia Ocean at Gnawnia, what MouseHunter will heed the call to stop the Dragon mouse from ravaging the Kingdom?

- 29 August 2012: The Dragon Mouse began dropping Ancient Relic as loot.
- Whenever the Marketplace is disabled, such as when recovering from a server outage, the Dragon Mouse is implicated with this message:

> A recent Dragon attack has left the marketplace slightly singed! We are currently doing maintenance on the marketplace.
Any marketpace items or unclaimed gold will be available when the marketplace returns.
Please check back later.

| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 105,000 | Gold: | 10,250 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Draconic | Other Requirements: | Draconic IslandDraconic Sanctuary |
| Cheese: | Cloud CheesecakeExtra Rich Cloud Cheesecake | Charm: |  |
| Locations: | Floating Islands | Loot: | Sky OreSky Glass |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Training at such high altitudes has acclimated these dragons by increasing their lung capacity allowing for more oxygen to burn when they breathe plumes of fire! Their fiery exhales eventually become so powerful that the sheer force thrusts them into the air, above their opponent, allowing them to hover in place! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1027 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

The Dragonbreather Mouse is only attracted to Cloud Cheesecake.

- On a Draconic Island or a Draconic Sanctuary, the Dragonbreather Mouse is only attracted to Cloud Cheesecake.
- In a Draconic Hoard, the Dragonbreather Mouse is only attracted to Extra Rich Cloud Cheesecake.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Draconic traps are effective against the Dragonbreather Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Dragonbreather Mouse can only be encountered on a Draconic Island, a Draconic Sanctuary, or a Draconic Hoard.

Dragonbane Charms, Super Dragonbane Charms and Extreme Dragonbane Charms provide a power bonus against the Dragonbreather Mouse.

## Loot

### Floating Islands's Island Based Loot

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Dragonbreather Mouse can drop the following loot:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Dragonbreather Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

| Those who defy the city are given the choice to redeem themselves by serving as its defenders within the dangerous Labyrinth or spend the rest of their short mortal lives in the bowels of the city. # 🐭 Dwarf Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Dwarf Mouse is a breed of mouse found in various locations in Gnawnia, Valour, Whisker Woods, Burroughs, and Furoma.

| Dwarf Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Indigenous Mice (Misc.) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 225 | Gold: | 450 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Any type | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Standard Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | BazaarCalm ClearingGreat Gnarled TreeHarbourKing's ArmsMeadowMountainTournament HallTown of DigbyTown of GnawniaTraining GroundsWindmill | Loot: | Packet of FlourPlankrun's Gnawnia Notes |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Often found living in remote areas, the dwarf mouse moves quickly, making it more difficult to catch than the basic coloured mouse. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 59 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Dwarf Mouse can be attracted using Standard Cheese.

In the Windmill location, the Dwarf Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Grilled Cheese.
In the Mountain location, the Dwarf Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Chedd-Ore Cheese or Abominable Asiago Cheese.
In the Calm Clearing location, the Dwarf Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Cherry Cheese.
In the Great Gnarled Tree location, the Dwarf Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Gnarled Cheese.

In the Bazaar location, the Dwarf Mouse can also be encountered when hunting with Gilded Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Dwarf Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All except Parental | None |

## Loot

The Dwarf Mouse drops various loot in the following locations:

- Town of Gnawnia

> Plankrun's Gnawnia Notes (drops only once if it is not in the Hunter's inventory)

- Windmill

> Packet of Flour

The Dwarf Mouse has not been found to drop loot in the Bazaar, Calm Clearing, Great Gnarled Tree, Harbour, King's Arms, Meadow, Mountain, Tournament Hall, Town of Digby, or Training Grounds locations.

## History and Trivia

- The Dwarf Mouse is one of the first mice that can be found and is generally the most common mouse encountered after the White, Brown, and Grey mice. In MouseHunt 2.0 when mice were assigned power types, it was usually the first Tactical type mouse encountered by hunters.
- January 2010: The Dwarf Mouse description was briefly changed for the 2010 New Year and references an event that occurred at the 2009 MTV Music Awards.

> Now that he's found all of this colorful loot, the Dwarf Mouse has developed a penchant for interrupting people during acceptance speeches. 2010 is the best New Year's. . . of all time.

- 8 June 2010: After the introduction of MouseHunt 3.0, the Dwarf Mouse was no longer found in the Lagoon and stopped dropping Splintered Wood in the Mountain.
- 30 June 2010: Prior to the introduction of the Windmill, the Dwarf Mouse dropped Plankrun's Gnawnia Notes in the Meadow rather than the Town of Gnawnia.
- 16 April 2011: The Dwarf Mouse began dropping the Antique Blue Argyle Egg as loot in the Town of Digby as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 25 June 2013: The Dwarf Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Rift Guardian Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Dwarf Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Dwarf Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 2 September 2014: The Dwarf Mouse began dropping Packets of Flour at the Windmill.
- 17 October 2018: The Dwarf Mouse was removed from the Laboratory.

## History and Trivia

- 14 April 2011: The Eagle Owl Mouse began dropping a bonus Whisker Woods Egg as loot as part of the annual Spring Egg Hunt.
- 16 April 2011: The Eagle Owl Mouse began dropping the Antique Blue Argyle Egg as loot in the Great Gnarled Tree as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 25 June 2013: The Eagle Owl Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law traps with the release of Varmint Valley.

| This mouse cares not for cheese. To say the Eclipse Mouse commands the forces of darkness would be to underestimate its power, for it commands the very fear of darkness, liquifying hunters’ souls with a constant barrage of nightmares. It also turns traps against their owners, sending knives, lasers and even giant squid sailing through the air after opponents. It has only been trapped once before, and after it reached the King’s gauntlet, the hunter disappeared. Good luck. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 61 |  |  |

## Hunting Strategy

Eclipse Mouse is only attracted to Gauntlet Cheese Tier 8.

### Power Type Weaknesses

Though several power types are shown as very effective, Forgotten is relatively much more effective against Eclipse Mouse.

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ForgottenPhysicalShadowTacticalArcaneHydroDraconic | None | Parental |

## Loot

Eclipse Mouse can drop Ancient Relic as loot.

## History and Trivia

- The "Stealth Mouse" in the 1 April 2010 news item was the silhouetted version of the Eclipse Mouse.
- 8 June 2010: Eclipse Mouse was released as part of King's Gauntlet location.
- 9 July 2010: The King granted permission to use the Gauntlet Potion Tier 8, allowing Hunters to hunt the Eclipse Mouse.
- 29 August 2012: The Eclipse Mouse began dropping Ancient Relic as loot.

The Eel Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

The Eel Mouse is susceptible only to Hydro weapons. It can be found in the Deep Oxygen Stream, Haunted Shipwreck, and Murky Depths zones while on a Dive in the Sunken City.

| This mindless construct is effectively a walking magic battery. Its technology allows it to travel around Zugzwang's library literally pulling the magic from ancient tomes. As the pure magic is absorbed, it is processed and stored within the magical shell of the mouse. Due to the imperfect nature of the magic refinement process it tends to look unstable, flaring into small bubbling sparks that give this mouse his odd name. Once an Effervescent Mouse has gathered enough magic it will report to its creator Zurreal the Eternal for siphoning. |  |  |  |

I defeated El Flamenco in a dizzying dance battle!
To commorate his first defeat, I earned a special gift from the King: A DLU+ Gift Box!
On subsequent encounters, this message changes to:
El Flamenco got served!
His conga line dissolves in confusion.
Additionally, after every catch, a new Conga Line is started with this message:
A conga line approaches!
Grim their eyes, heavy their feet,
a lockstep whirlwind of defeat.

- 22 April 2009: The Elven Princess Mouse began appearing in the Lagoon location.
- 4 April 2012: The Elven Princess Mouse began dropping a bonus Whisker Woods Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event.
- 25 June 2013: The Elven Princess Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.

# 🐭 Emberstone Scaled Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Emberstone Scaled Mouse is a breed of mouse found in Queso Geyser.

| Emberstone Scaled Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Queso Canyoneers (Pressure Builder) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 300,000 | Gold: | 60,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Draconic | Other Requirements: | Building Pressure |
| Cheese: | Wildfire Queso | Charm: |  |
| Locations: | Queso Geyser | Loot: | Ancient RelicWild Tonic |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This mouse heats up its huge, stonelike scales to vaporize pools of queso around the geyser, generating immense pressure. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 984 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Emberstone Scaled Mouse is only attracted to Wildfire Queso.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Emberstone Scaled Mouse.

## Hunting Strategy

Catching an Emberstone Scaled Mouse while building pressure in Queso Geyser increases the amount of pressure by 1,500 kPa.

Only Draconic traps are effective against the Emberstone Scaled Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Emberstone Scaled Mouse can only be encountered while building pressure in Queso Geyser, after a cork has been constructed.

Dragonbane Charm, Super Dragonbane Charm, Extreme Dragonbane Charm, and Ultimate Dragonbane Charm provide a power bonus against the Emberstone Scaled Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 30 April 2019: The Emberstone Scaled Mouse was introduced, with the release of the Queso Geyser area.

The Ethereal Librarian is a breed of mouse that can be found in the Lost City, and is known to drop Aleth Essences, Ber Essences, Cynd Essences and Dreamfluff Herbs as loot.

| Ethereal Librarian |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Lost City Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 14,500 | Gold: | 1,850 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Arcane | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Dewthief Camembert | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Lost City | Loot: | Aleth EssenceBer EssenceCynd EssenceDreamfluff Herbs |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Always arguing that ancient and forbidden texts should be opened and revealed, this mouse and his constant quest to unearth secrets best left buried is a prime candidate as to why the city was lost in the first place. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 423 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Ethereal Librarian has been found to be only attracted to Dewthief Camembert.

## Hunting Strategy

The Ethereal Librarian is a member of the Lost City mouse group. As such all power types except Arcane weapons are ineffective against it.

## History and Trivia

- 14 November 2012: The Ethereal Librarian was released as a part of the Living Garden update.

Evil Scientist Mouse can be attracted using any Standard String Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Evil Scientist Mouse can be attracted when the Mist Meter is in No Mist Zone.

# 🐭 Excitable Electric Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Excitable Electric Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnia Rift location. They are known to drop Riftiago Potions as loot.

| Excitable Electric Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Rift Walkers (Gnawnia Rift) |  |  |  |

| Lightning storms within the Rift are unlikely to be caused by an actual weather system. Instead, concentrated electrical anomalies on the surface charge up the air. Closer inspection of these anomalies has shown that they originate from within the mice themselves!Coursing with electric currents, this mouse acts like a reverse lightning rod. His shocking subatomic particles allow him to absorb and store large amounts of electric ions from the atmosphere into which he can easily release with a wave of his hand!" |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 563 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Excitable Electric mice have been found to be especially attracted to String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Excitable Electric Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

### Other Events

- 2015

> Great Winter Hunt 2015

- 2014

> Halloween 2014

## History and Trivia

- Excitable Electric mice were introduced on 25 March 2014, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- Excitable Electric mice are the Rift version of Lightning Rod mice.

| The Tech District's strongest weakest mouse! The Exo-Tech has developed an advanced exo-skeleton to augment his older, feeble body. By flexing his body's strongest muscle, his mind, he can hit as hard and fast as solving quantum entanglement theory! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 759 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Exo-Tech Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

## Hunting Strategy

Forgotten weapons are very effective against the Exo-Tech Mouse. Arcane weapons are of normal effectiveness.

The Exo-Tech Mouse can be found in the Tech Foundry Outskirts, the Tech Research Center, and the Manaforge.

## Loot

- Labyrinth Theme Scrap I and Tech Power Cores

## Event Participation

- The Exo-Tech Mouse drops the Zokor Tech Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

> 9 September 2015: The Exo-Tech Mouse was introduced as part of the Hollow Heights release.

Zugzwang's magical library is so expansive that parts of it are still unexplored; it is much bigger on the inside than on the outside. Legions of these mice are created to travel the great halls and map the tower, but their efforts continue to fail. Either the creations are not intelligent enough to map out the library effectively, or the library's magics make it impossible to reach the ends. |  |  |  |

# 🐭 Fairy Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Fairy Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Forest Guild |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 5,775 | Gold: | 930 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Tactical | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Standard CheeseGnarled CheeseWicked Gnarly Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Great Gnarled TreeLagoon | Loot: | Map of the Lagoon |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Do not let their size and mild temperament fool you: Fairy mice love cheese and can easily dodge a trap in order to get it. Lore states that these mice travel so quickly that they can instantly appear anywhere. Thus, when a Hunter’s cheese disappears in the blink of an eye, they often blame the Fairies. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 69 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Fairy Mouse is attracted only to the following cheeses:

- Great Gnarled Tree

> Standard Cheese
Gnarled Cheese

- Lagoon

> Gnarled Cheese
Wicked Gnarly Cheese

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Fairy Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | PhysicalShadowArcaneHydroLaw | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Fairy Mouse in the Great Gnarled Tree and Lagoon locations.

Hunters must capture the Fairy Mouse in the Great Gnarled Tree in order to obtain the Map of the Lagoon and gain access to the Lagoon location.

## Loot

The Fairy Mouse drops the Map of the Lagoon in the Great Gnarled Tree location.

The Fairy Mouse is not known to drop any loot in the Lagoon location.

## History and Trivia

- 9 April 2009: The Fairy Mouse was released with the introduction of the Great Gnarled Tree.
- 22 April 2009: The Fairy Mouse begins dropping Map of the Lagoon as loot, and begins appearing in the Lagoon.
- 25 June 2013: The Fairy Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law traps with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 29 July 2015: The strength of the Fairy Mouse was reduced and it's chance of encounter was increased.

# 🐭 Fall Familiar Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Fall Familiar Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Rodentia region. It can be attracted and caught only during the Fall Season.

| Fall Familiar Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Seasonal Soldiers (Fall) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 9,940 | Gold: | 830 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Shadow | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | SUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Seasonal Garden | Loot: | Ancient Relic |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| The dry rustle of leaves blowing in the wind accompanies the passing of the Fall Familiar, bringing with it the smell of lit jack-o-lanterns. The sky is always a little dusky as it roams the garden, which provides ample cover for his minions to rob hunters attempting to track him. Although it never usually gets its gnarled paws dirty with such things, the Fall Familiar is quite an accomplished thief, able to turn into a swirl of dead leaves if it is in danger of being caught. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 243 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Fall Familiar mice have been found to be especially attracted to SUPER|brie+ cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

Strong Shadow weapons such as Clockapult of Time are recommended in order to catch this mouse. Alternatively, hunters may equip their best Tactical trap.

## History and Trivia

- Fall Familiar Mice were introduced on 17 August 2010 with the release of the Seasonal Garden location.
- On 27 March 2011 they began to drop the Sandtail Call to Arms for the first time, following the release of the Fiery Warpath location on 22 March 2011.
- Fall Familiar Mice began dropping Ancient Relic as loot on 29 August 2012.
- On 29 July 2015 they stopped dropping the Sandtail Call to Arms as loot with the introduction of the Adventure Book.

| The Fetid Swamp mouse gives off a foul odour which wilts nearby plant life. Its forced existence within swamps, among these wilted plants, has given this mouse an interesting penchant for Crunchy Havarti cheese. |  |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Fetid Swamp mice have been found to be especially attracted to Crunchy Havarti cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

Shadow type traps such as the Clockapult of Time are very effective against Fetid Swamp mice. All traps with other power types, including Arcane type traps such as the Obelisk of Slumber, are ineffective against these mice.

## History and Trivia

- On 13 December 2009, Fetid Swamp Mice dropped the Laboratory Map Piece for the first time.
- 29 July 2015: The Fetid Swamp Mouse stopped dropping Laboratory Map Piece.

| Using only a fiddle and a whole lot of sentiment, Fiddler Mice play some of the most heart-rending songs ever heard from a member of the rodent family. # 🐭 Field Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Field Mouse is a breed of mouse found in various locations in Meadow and Windmill.

| Field Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Indigenous Mice (Misc.) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 200 | Gold: | 200 |

| Locations: | MeadowWindmill | Loot: | Marble CheesePacket of FlourSatchel of Gold (500) |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| After finding a tuft of grass in a private location, usually by a nice view, visitors to Gnawnia and the Meadow often decide to spread out blankets and have a picnic. Unfortunately, when they try to sit down, they realize that their feast is being carried off by a multitude of mice who have posed as grass to lure them in. Because of these recent scams, the King has advised the public to be extra wary of scenic vistas, charming fruit groves, and any and all topiary. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 230 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Field Mouse is attracted only to Standard Cheese.

In the Windmill location, the Field Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Grilled Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Field Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |

## Loot

The Field Mouse drops various loot in the following locations:

- Meadow:

> Marble Cheese
Satchel of Gold (500)

- Windmill

> Packet of Flour

## History and Trivia

- 30 June 2010: The Field Mouse was introduced as part of the Windmill update.
- 25 June 2013: The Field Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Field Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 25 March 2014: The Goliath Field Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Field Mouse.

# 🐭 Firebreather Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Firebreather Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Rodentia region. It can be attracted and caught only during the Summer Season.

| Firebreather Mouse |  |  |  |

| Some mice use fire for the sake of wanton destruction, but to the Firebreather mouse, destruction is an art. One graceful puff from their torch, and your cheese is fondue. Another spiraling fireball later, your pants are on fire. Then, after a flourish and a wave of the paw, there's a ring of flame around you that you're not sure how to get rid of. Not surprisingly, Hunters' lore advises all visitors to the Summer island to skip the Firebreather's floor show. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 244 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Firebreather Mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

Tactical weapons such as Zugzwang's First Move should be used in order to catch this mouse.

- Firebreather Mice were introduced on 17 August 2010 with the release of the Seasonal Garden location.
- Firebreather Mice began dropping Sandtail Call to Arms on 22 March 2011 with the release of the Fiery Warpath location.
- On 29 July 2015 they stopped dropping the Sandtail Call to Arms as loot with the introduction of the Adventure Book.

- 30 June 2010: The Flying Mouse was added to the Meadow and Windmill locations.
- 25 June 2013: The Flying Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 19 September 2013: The Flying Mouse gained a taste for Brie Cheese and Gouda Cheese during the Gnawnia population shift, although SUPER|brie+ still remains the most effective bait.
- 25 March 2014: The Dream Drifter Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Flying Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Flying Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 2 September 2014: The Flying Mouse began dropping Packet of Flour as loot at the Windmill.
- 5 October 2021: The Gold for Flying Mouse was increased as part of the Town of Gnawnia and Windmill gameplay update.

# 🐭 Force Fighter

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

During MouseHunt's Fifth Birthday, five new mice decided to form an attack force against Ronza's airship. Known as the Force Fighter Five, each of these mice had their own colored costume, and each dropped loot of multiple Force Fighter Action Figures matching their color. Additionally, the mice could join forces into a larger Super FighterBot MegaSupreme robot, terrorizing the event.

Thankfully, the mice were careless, and also dropped Robot Scraps as loot. A hunter could collect 10 of each color of scrap, as well as buy a Repurposed Robot Head from Ronza, in order to create the Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap. This weapon was the only way to beat the combined Force Fighters, and earn the Crushed Birthday Cake Base as a reward for saving the birthday celebrations.

| Mouse | Collectible | Scrap |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Force Fighter Blue | Force Fighter Blue Action Figure | Blue Leg Robot Scrap |
| Force Fighter Green | Force Fighter Green Action Figure | Green Arm Robot Scrap |
| Force Fighter Pink | Force Fighter Pink Action Figure | Pink Leg Robot Scrap |
| Force Fighter Red | Force Fighter Red Action Figure | Red Chest Robot Scrap |
| Force Fighter Yellow | Force Fighter Yellow Action Figure | Yellow Arm Robot Scrap |

Progress in building the new weapon could be tracked in the Heads-up Display at Ronza's Traveling Shoppe. Upon catching the tenth mouse of any color, this additional message appeared in the Hunter's Journal:

> I've collected all the XXX Robot Scraps needed for a fully-functional super-robot XXX!

After collecting all 50 scraps, another message appeared in the Hunter's Journal:

> I've collected all the robot scraps I can! I should visit Ronza to turn all this into a powerful super-robot trap!

After a hunter has all 50 scraps and the Repurposed Robot Head, the Heads-up Display adds a message "You've got the parts! Complete the trap in Ronza's Shoppe!"

After buying the Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap, the Heads-Up Display changes to a picture of the two robots facing one another, initially with a message "Arm your Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap to fight back!". There is also a Boss meter at the bottom of the Heads-up Display, with a hint that states "Each Super FighterBot MegaSupreme catch does 10 damage to their HP! The Super FighterBot MegaSupreme has 100/100 HP."

After arming the Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap, the original five Force Fighter mice cannot be attracted, but it is then possible to attract the Super FighterBot MegaSupreme mouse. Each failed hunt that attracted the Super FighterBot MegaSupreme shows an animation of it firing a barrage of missiles at the hunter; and each successful catch shows an animation of the hunter's MegaMouser charging the FighterBot with a large explosion. Each catch weakens the FighterBot by 10 HP, with the following message in the Hunter's Journal:

> Your rocket-powered fist connected solidly, dealing 10 damage, leaving the robot with only XX HP remaining!

Finally, after defeating the FighterBot 10 times, the hunter was awarded the Crushed Birthday Cake Base, along with this message in the journal:

> Your super-robot has proven to be more powerful, defeating and demoralizing the Mouse Force Five! Their main charge has been blunted and Ronza has been saved!
With their spirit broken, the Mouse Force Five are now simply watching from the sidelines and no longer actively attacking Ronza. Who would, with you protecting her?
With the robot threat deterred, you finally have time to collect your, ah, slightly crushed reward.
You pick up the following loot:
1 Crushed Birthday Cake Base

Thereafter, a third Heads-up Display background is shown, with a View again link for replaying the final battle.

### Force Fighter Action Figure

> Oh wow, these... These aren't particularly well-made action figures, are they? The paint's not quite the right colour, the male bodies and the female bodies are exactly the same, they're missing critical costume features, and - ow! That's a splinter!
I guess the reason the Mouse Force Five have so many of these on-hand is that nobody will buy them.

All five action figure collectibles (blue, green, pink, red, and yellow) have the same description. There was no limit on the amount a hunter could collect. The Event Scoreboard allowed hunters to compare who had collected the most.

During the annual MouseHunt Birthday, Hunters were able to trade in these collectibles for Party Charms at the event Charm Shoppe, but have been rendered obsolete since the conclusion of Birthday Party Zone, Mousehunt's Ninth Birthday

### Robot Scrap

> This scrap may not look like much now, but collect enough of them, and you can build a super-robot of your own to challenge the Mouse Force Five!

All five scrap types (blue leg, green arm, pink leg, red chest, and yellow arm) have the same description. A hunter could own at most 10 of a given color scrap at the same time.

### Repurposed Robot Head

> It's amazing what you can do with the scrap laying around - so long as it's high-quality scrap, anyway. And who would have higher-quality scrap than Ronza?
Made from spare trap parts and coated in melted gold accumulated through years of fantastic sales, this head should slot right to complete your Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap.

This head could be purchased from Ronza's Traveling Shoppe General Store for 100,000 gold, and could be returned for the same value. A hunter could own at most one at a time.

## History and Trivia

- The Travel map showed images of the Super FighterBot MegaSupreme emerging from the Harbour and approaching the eventual event site, starting on 4 March 2013.
- The Force Fighter Five were introduced on 7 March 2013.
- These mice are based on the Power Rangers, a popular television series.

### Cheese

In the Calm Clearing location, the Foxy Mouse is only attracted when hunting with SUPER|brie+.
In the Great Gnarled Tree location, the Foxy Mouse will be attracted when hunting with Standard Cheese and Gnarled Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Foxy Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | PhysicalShadowArcaneHydroLaw | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Foxy Mouse in the Calm Clearing location (though it is extremely rare) and in the Great Gnarled Tree location.

## Loot

The Foxy Mouse drops Swiss Cheese as loot in the Calm Clearing and Great Gnarled Tree locations.

## History and Trivia

- 31 March 2009: The Foxy Mouse was released with the introduction of the Calm Clearing.
- 8 June 2010: With the MouseHunt 3.0 update, the Foxy Mouse gained a preference for SUPER|brie+ in the Calm Clearing.
- 25 June 2013: The Foxy Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law traps with the release of Varmint Valley.

# 🐭 Frost King

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Frost King is an Event Mouse available during annual Great Winter Hunt events.

| Frost King |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (Great Winter Hunt) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |

| This once warm and cheery fellow has always enjoyed the festive season... perhaps a little too much. As a youngster, he would look forward to the holidays all year long but when they finally arrived, all he could think of was how soon they would be gone again. Over time, the warmth that he once felt for this magical season was replaced by an icy cold sliver in his heart that grew and grew until it eventually consumed him, transforming him completely from head to toe.<br>Driven mad by obsession, he became convinced that he could capture the magic of the holidays and keep it forever simply by freezing the festive season in place. And so he set to work on a plan to usher in an infinite winter to the Kingdom of Gnawnia.<br>By harnessing the power of his Magical Holiday Scarf, the self-proclaimed Frost King conjured a fortress made of Reinforced Ice and constructed the nefarious Frost Globes that would allow him to turn this luscious landscape into a frozen prison from which the festive season could never escape! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1125 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture Frost King can be found on the current Great Winter Hunt location page.

## History and Trivia

- Frost King is listed as Naughty on Naughty List Printing Press Base image.

# 🐭 Frostbite Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Frostbite Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Rodentia region. It can be attracted and caught only during the Winter Season. They are not known to drop any loot.

| Frostbite Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Seasonal Soldiers (Winter) |  |  |  |

| Points: | 11,400 | Gold: | 5,040 |

| Locations: | Seasonal Garden | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Sharp as the biting wind on a January morning, the Frostbite mouse embodies the cold of a deep, northern winter. Frost feeds its powers, allowing it to use the magic of Zugzwang's garden to become stronger and sharper than ever before. At the peak of its power, the Frostbite mouse's claws become as hard as diamonds, and freeze their victims on contact. Don't expect any fear from a Frostbite mouse, either-- their disposition is as unyielding as a glacier. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 246 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Frostbite Mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Strong Hydro weapons such as Ancient Spear Gun and Heat Bath are recommended in order to catch this mouse. Alternatively, hunters may equip their best Tactical trap.

## Event Participation

- The Frostbite Mouse drops the Seasonal Garden Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Frostbite Mice were introduced on 17 August 2010 with the release of the Seasonal Garden location.
- During the Spring Egg Hunt 2011, Frostbite mice began dropping a bonus Seasonal Garden Egg as loot for hunters who had obtained the Rocket Rabbit Mallet. They dropped the same loot the following year during the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event for hunters who had obtained the Eggscavator.

# 🐭 Ful'Mina the Mountain Queen

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Ful'Mina, The Mountain Queen is a breed of mouse found in Moussu Picchu.

| Ful'Mina the Mountain Queen |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Moussu Picchu Inhabitants (Storm Dragon) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 120,000 | Gold: | 30,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Draconic | Other Requirements: | High-Max both Wind and Rain intensity (80%-100%) |
| Cheese: | Dragonvine Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Moussu Picchu | Loot: | Ancient RelicArcanevineBaitkeep CharmCavern FungusCrystal CrucibleDamaged OculusDivine OrbDragon EmberDragon ScalesEmpowered Brie CheeseEmpowered SUPER&#124;brie+Enigmatic CoreEssence of DestructionEssence PrismFlawless OrbFul'Mina's ToothGalleon GoudaGauntlet Potion Tier 5Gauntlet Potion Tier 6Gauntlet Potion Tier 7Gauntlet Potion Tier 8Glowing Gruyere CheeseGold LeafInferno Havarti CheeseInfused PlateIonized SaltLantern OilLiving ShardMagic Essence Meteoric Core FragmentsMeteorite PieceMineralsMoon CheeseMoussu Picchu Theme Scrap IIINightshadeOasis BeadPollutinum Powercore HammerPredatory ProcessorRainy PotionRare Map DustRumble CheeseSacred ScriptSandblasted MetalScrap MetalShadowvineShard of GlassSimple OrbSphynx CrystalTemporal Shadow PlateTower ManaUltimate Attraction CharmUltimate CharmUltimate Luck CharmUltimate Lucky Power CharmUltimate Power CharmUltimate Spore CharmUnstable CharmVengeful Vanilla Stilton CheeseWindy Potion |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Resting high above Moussu Picchu among the clouds, Ful'Mina is the true queen of the mountain and ruler over the skies and storms. As the Moussu Picchu Inhabitants below battle endlessly for dominance of the weather, the resulting stalemate allows the queen to slumber for generations.<br>Once, long ago, she would appear high above the land anywhere a lightning cloud hovered, visiting the many realms of mice across Gnawnia, her tail smacking the ground like lightning as she plucked treasures with it to add to her hoard. Now, countless decades pass in between sightings of the Mountain Queen, so much so that her very existence has gone from folklore, to legend, to mere myth...<br>But now, MouseHunters from the Kingdom of Gnawnia have found their way through the mountain pass, and their presence has brought a shift to the power struggle in these ruins, as they always do. Hunters have begun to harness the powers of both the Wind Wanderer and Rain Roamer tribes of mice in the area, ironically bringing unity between the two after endless centuries of feuding.<br>As the winds grow stronger, the rains fall heavier, and the storms begin to mix and clash, great bone-shaking growls can be heard from above the thickest of clouds, where the queen is said to reside. Is this thunder? Or is it the great Mountain Queen being rudely awakened from her slumber? Slowly but surely, disturbed by the intensifying battle in the lands below and the low rumble of impending thunderstorms in the clouds upon which she rests, she begins to rouse from her deep sleep...<br>Soon, and without warning, Ful'Mina, the Mountain Queen, will descend upon the lands with great fury to bring order to the chaos through the destructive force of her indomitable lightning storms. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 891 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Ful'Mina, The Mountain Queen is only attracted to Dragonvine Cheese.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Draconic | None | None |

## Hunting Strategy

Only Draconic type traps are effective against Ful'Mina, The Mountain Queen, all other power types are ineffective.

Dragonbane Charm, Super Dragonbane Charm, Extreme Dragonbane Charm, and Ultimate Dragonbane Charm provide a power bonus against the Ful'Mina the Mountain Queen.

Ful'Mina, The Mountain Queen is only available when both wind and rain intensities are at least 80% in Moussu Picchu.

## History and Trivia

- 6 September 2017: Ful'Mina, The Mountain Queen was introduced as part of the Moussu Picchu release.

# 🐭 Gargantuamouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Gargantuamouse is a breed of mouse found in the Fiery Warpath as part of the first three waves. It is known to drop Ancient Relic and Flameshard as loot.

| Gargantuamouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: The Marching Flame (Support) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 25,000 | Gold: | 4,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Draconic | Other Requirements: | Streak at 7 or higher |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Fiery Warpath | Loot: | Ancient RelicFlameshard |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| There are two things known for certain about the Gargantuamouse. The first is that it is the only known predator of the Dragon Mouse. The second is that only a Warmonger has ever managed to harness its power. If the battle is not going well, a Warmonger will not hesitate to unleash these Draconic beasts. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 305 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Gargantuamouse can be attracted by any Standard Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The Gargantuamouse can be attracted when the Hunter's Streak is at 7 or higher in Fiery Warpath. Its attraction rate increases when the Streak gets higher.

The Hunter can use Gargantua Charm to remove all mice except the Gargantuamouse from the attraction pool when the required Streak is reached, thus guarantees the next attraction being the Gargantuamouse.

Draconic power type is very effective against this mouse. Arcane, Physical, Tactical, and Hydro are less effective against it.

When the Hunter fails to capture a Gargantuamouse, the following message will appear on the Hunter's Journal:

> Fearing that I was gaining the upper hand, the Warmonger released a Gargantuamouse, putting an end to my streak.

## History and Trivia

- 22 March 2011: The Gargantuamouse was released as part of the Fiery Warpath.
- 29 August 2012: The Gargantuamouse began dropping Ancient Relic as loot.

| This fearless rogue travels the countryside slaying monsters for coin using a combination of magic and swordsmanship. He trained hard to be as skilled as he is by completing the perilous Trial of the Cheeses before becoming a pupil at the School of the Cat. Common folk often fear and despise him but that doesn't stop him from protecting them from the likes of Basilisks and Bruxas. Next time you see one of these unsung heroes pass through your village, remember to toss a coin to your Mouser.<br>The only thing that can distract this mouse from its fantastical tale is the pleasant aroma of Plotting Paneer Cheese. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1259 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Gerald Rivers is only attracted to Plotting Paneer.

## Hunting Strategy

The Gerald Rivers can only be attracted while writing a story.

Only Law power types are effective against Gerald Rivers.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 13 January 2026: Gerald Rivers was released as part of Conclusion Cliffs location.

## Loot

The Ghost Mouse drops Stale Cheese in the Catacombs location.

The Ghost Mouse will drop Ectoplasmic Essence in the Mousoleum location when working the Zurreal Trap Research Assignment.
Three Ectoplasmic Essences are necessary for crafting the Zurreal's Folly weapon.

The Ghost Mouse is not known to drop any other loot in the Mousoleum or Forbidden Grove locations.

## History and Trivia

- 10 July 2008: The Ghost Mouse is released for the first time.
- 12 January 2011: The Ghost Mouse could be found at Ronza's Traveling Shoppe where it dropped Rockforth Cheese as loot.
- 14 April 2011: The Ghost Mouse began dropping the Antique Green Plaid Egg as loot in the Catacombs as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 23 August 2011: The Ghost Mouse began to drop Ectoplasmic Essence as loot in the Mousoleum while hunters are in the process of completing the Zurreal Trap Research Assignment.
- 25 March 2013: The Ghost Mouse began dropping the Haunted Egg as part of the annual Spring Egg Hunt event.

## History and Trivia

- When MouseHunt experiences unexpected downtime, the Glitchpaw Mouse is released as a treat for Hunters upon the game returning.
- When Hunters encounter issues accessing the game, the image of the Glitchpaw Mouse may appear along with an error message similar to:

> Uh oh! A Glitchpaw mouse sabotaged our code!
HitGrab developers have been notified of your problem.
Error Code:

- 1 April 2009: The Glitchpaw Mouse was briefly released for the first time. It could not be found in the Bristle Woods region, the Meditation Room nor the Pinnacle Chamber.
- 28 July 2009: The Glitchpaw Mouse dropped SUPER|brie+ as loot for the first time.
- 19 February 2010: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released.
- 13 July 2010: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released in all locations, dropping location specific loot and cheese.
- 14 January 2011: The Glitchpaw Mousewas released in all locations.
- 14-23 August 2011: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released after six hours of unscheduled maintenance.
- 7-12 March 2012: The Glitchpaw Mouse was available across nearly the entire Kingdom for the remainder of MouseHunt's Fourth Birthday Celebration.
- 15-19 February 2013: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released during the Year of the Snake Festival, due to unscheduled server downtime.
- 20 March 2013: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released for a few days after a planned server upgrade caused a nine hour downtime.
- 11 April 2013: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released after unplanned downtime interfered with the Spring Egg Hunt 2013 and was available for the remainder of the event along with +5 Luck bonus.
- 4 September 2013: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released for a few days due to unscheduled server downtime.
- 11-14 October 2013: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to unscheduled server downtime, making their first appearance in Claw Shot City.
- 13 December 2013: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to unscheduled server downtime to fix tournament glitches during the Great Winter Hunt 2013.
- 7 March 2014: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to unscheduled server downtime during MouseHunt's Sixth Birthday.
- 13 April 2014: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to unplanned server downtime.
- 21-29 October 2014: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to intermittent server downtime.
- 4-5 March 2015: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to intermittent server downtime.
- 3 September 2015: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to unplanned server downtime.
- 27 May 2016: The Glitchpaw Mouse was released due to unplanned server downtime.

Gold: 1,200 -> 2,404
Old description:

> The diet of a Glitchpaw mouse mostly consists of wires and circuit boards. Although they spend most of their time in hiding, occasionally these mice run rampant throughout the Kingdom causing all sorts of technical problems for the King's hunting operation. Is your cheese failing to attract? Can't seem to find that oh-so-rare map piece? Having a horrible streak of "red boxes"? It's a pretty safe bet that you can blame all of your problems on these scapegoats!

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Gluttonous Zombie Mouse is attracted only to Moon Cheese, Radioactive Blue Cheese, Undead Emmental and Undead String Emmental in Catacombs.The Gluttonous Zombie Mouse is not attracted to Ancient Cheese in Catacombs.

The Gluttonous Zombie Mouse is attracted only to Crimson Cheese, Radioactive Blue Cheese, Undead Emmental and Undead String Emmental in Mousoleum.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Gluttonous Zombie Mouse. Arming Brain Charm or Super Brain Charm increases the chance of encountering the Gluttonous Zombie Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Shadow | ArcaneForgotten | PhysicalTactical |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Gluttonous Zombie Mouse in the Catacombs and Mousoleum locations.

## Loot

The Gluttonous Zombie Mouse is known to drop Brain Bits in any location when hunting with the Limited Edition Brain Extractor or Maniacal Brain Extractor traps or Skello-ton base.

The Gluttonous Zombie Mouse is known to drop Cemetery Slat, Wealth Charm, Super Wealth Charm and Ultimate Wealth Charm in Mousoleum.

The Gluttonous Zombie Mouse is not known to drop any other loot in Catacombs.

Attraction info and special event loot drops for the Gluttonous Zombie Mouse can be found at the individual event location pages.

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Gold Mouse can be attracted using Standard Cheese.

In the Town of Gnawnia location, the Gold Mouse can also be encountered when hunting with Gilded Cheese.
In the Windmill location, the Gold Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Grilled Cheese.
In the Mountain location, the Gold Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Chedd-Ore Cheese or Abominable Asiago Cheese.
In the Dojo location, the Gold Mouse can also be encountered when hunting with Maki Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Gold Mouse.

Hunters may arm the Prospector's Charm in the Mountain location to improve the attraction rate of the Gold Mouse, Diamond Mouse, and Silvertail Mouse.

The Gold Mouse is not known to drop any loot in the King's Arms, Tournament Hall, or Town of Gnawnia locations.

## History and Trivia

- The visual appearance of the Gold Mouse bears a resemblance to the Budai figure from Chinese culture.
- 25 January 2011: The Gold Mouse began to drop Simple Orb as loot.
- 25 June 2013: The Gold Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Wealth Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Gold Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Gold Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 2 September 2014: The Gold Mouse began dropping Packet of Flour at the Windmill.
- 17 October 2018: The Gold Mouse changed mouse groups from Indigenous Mice to Mountain Mice.
- 5 October 2021: The Gold for Gold Mouse was increased as part of the Town of Gnawnia and Windmill gameplay updates.

# 🐭 Goliath Field Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Goliath Field Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnia Rift location. They are known to drop Ancient Relics, Divine Orbs and Raw Rift Crystals as loot.

| Goliath Field Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Rift Walkers (Gnawnia Rift) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 119,000 | Gold: | 15,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Rift | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Resonator | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Gnawnia Rift | Loot: | Ancient RelicDivine OrbRaw Rift Crystal |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Sleeping for centuries at a time, the Goliath Field mouse is a Rift wonder in itself. The Rift energies flow through the Goliath mice, allowing life to grow around them as they slumber. Some believe their dreams influence what grows on them. A Rift-seeking hunter would be incredibly lucky to see one moving in his lifetime.You'll feel a tremble at first, followed by seismic shaking. That low rumbling off in the distance turns into a roar as mountains crash together all around you! That's when you know... it has awakened. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 558 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Goliath Field mice have been found to be especially attracted to Resonator cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

Like all mice in the Gnawnia Rift, Goliath Field mice can be caught with any power type except Parental. However, due to all types except Rift being less effective against the mice in the area, it is in a hunter's best interest to focus on catching one Goliath Field as soon as possible, to gain the Crystal Tower trap (and the Riftwalker Set bonus if the hunter already has the Rift Base), to make all mice in the area easier to catch. If a hunter does not want to use an Ultimate charm, then Ultimate Power Charms coupled with the hunter's strongest non-Rift trap show a remarkable improvement in catch rate compared to any other charm.

Catching a hunter's first Goliath Field Mouse leaves a message in the Hunter's Journal:

> With the capture of the Goliath Field Mouse, the Gnawnia Rift lost a significant amount of its power.
The entrance to the Rift appears to have shrunk, making the Kingdom a bit safer from the threat of this Rift. Despite the fact no mouse can currently leave this Rift, I shall continue to endeavour to return when needed, hunting the mice here to ensure this Rift never tears wide open again!
Although I have saved the Kingdom for the time being, I fear that in time Rifts will open in other areas of the Kingdom.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Goliath Field Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Goliath Field mice were introduced on 25 March 2014, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- Goliath Field mice are the Rift version of Field mice.
- Goliath Field mouse is potentially the heaviest mouse found in the game when caught.


---

Despite his impressive magical might, he prefers to spend his time frolicking with mice smaller than him and entertaining them with fireworks and various other simple magic tricks. However, when his friends feel threatened, he becomes a force to be reckoned with, putting himself in harm's way and ensuring that anything that tries to get through him, shall not pass!<br>The only thing that can distract this mouse from its fantastical tale is the sweet scent of Storied Saga Cheese. |  |  |  |

## History and Trivia

- 31 March 2009: The Granite Mouse began dropping the Whisker Woods Clearing Map Piece in the Harbour, giving access to the Calm Clearing.
- 25 June 2013: The Granite Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Shard Centurion Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Granite Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Granite Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the release of the Rift Plane.
- 29 July 2015: The Whisker Woods Clearing Map Piece is renamed Calm Clearing Map Piece.
- 11 April 2017: The Granite Mouse stopped dropping the Calm Clearing Map Piece when the Harbour was updated.
- 17 October 2018: The Granite Mouse changed mouse groups from Indigenous Mice to Mountain Mice and was removed from the Laboratory.

## Hunting Strategy

Law traps are very effective against the Grave Robber Mouse. All other power types (except Rift and Parental) are of normal effectiveness and a more powerful trap can easily outperform the relatively weak Law traps.

The Grave Robber Mouse is available throughout the year in the Mousoleum and Catacombs locations, but only if a Hunter arms Undead Emmental cheese.

## History and Trivia

- 8 July 2011: The Grave Robber Mouse was painted live during an 'All about the art' Feedback Friday. Ideas and suggestions for how the mouse should look were provided by the audience at the time of painting.
- 8 July 2011: The Grave Robber Mouse was introduced as part of the second Zombie Invasion Weekend event.
- 25 June 2013: The Grave Robber Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.

| One greedy mouse took all the gifts this season.<br>Now, please don't ask why. No one quite knows the reason.<br>Perhaps he never quite got what he wanted,<br>While others that did just looked on and taunted.<br>Paul always got everything on his festive list,<br>But this mouse's wishes were just ignored and missed.<br>"Now, why do I never get what I ask?",<br>he yelled out loud with a huff and a gasp.<br>In a rage, he went out and stole someone's gift,<br>It was easy and fun and boy, were they miffed!<br>His eyes lit up, he knew just what to do!<br>"I'll go to Paul's house and steal his gifts too!"<br>But he didn't stop there, this was just the beginning,<br>"I must steal every gift!", he considered while grinning.<br>So off he went, down their chimneys with glee,<br>Grabbing all the gifts from under each tree.<br>The children will awake to a tree that is bare,<br>They'll cry and they'll scream, "Why, this just isn't fair!"<br>So be brave on your journey while hunting this mouse,<br>You must return all the gifts to each empty house.<br>Gnawnia needs you to put a stop to this stunt,<br>So we wish you good luck and a Great Winter Hunt! |  |  |  |

- 25 December 2012: Greedy Al was unlocked and could be caught by Hunters only after the construction of a Wrapped Gift Trap. When caught, Greedy Al wrote the following message in the Hunter's Journal:

> I gave Greedy Al his childhood presents!
A single tear rolled down his mousey cheek and he smiled at me.
He was so thrilled that he decided to remain behind and hand out presents to any mice who visit my trap! He thanked me for everything I had done and for renewing his spirit for the season!

The Grey Mouse prefers Standard Cheese, but will avoid traps baited with White Cheddar Cheese.

In the Windmill location, the Grey Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with White Cheddar Cheese or Grilled Cheese.

## Preferred Cheese

Grizzled Silth mice have been found to be especially attracted to String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice. They also will not appear unless a Stagnant Charm is armed.

## Hunting Strategy

The Grizzled Silth Mouse is weak to Rift Traps. The Grizzled Silth Mouse can only be attracted by String Cheese and Stagnant Charm. Catching the Grizzled Silth Mouse will award 6 Rage towards the Deep Lagoon section of the Whisker Woods Rift.

The Grizzled Silth Mouse is available at any rage in Whisker Woods Rift.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Grizzled Silth Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg and the Grizzled Silth Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Grizzled Silth mice were introduced on 28 January 2015, with the introduction of the Whisker Woods Rift.
- Grizzled Silth mice are the Rift version of Silth mice.

| The School of Sorcery has a long and exhaustive list of rules that are constantly being amended and extended. These rules are particularly strict with regards to loitering and lollygagging in the halls between classes. Since the detention dungeon is the last place that any student would want to find themselves, there is only one mouse that you will ever find roaming the halls at this school and that's the Hall Monitor.<br>Armed with the latest edition of the rules compendium and their signature silver mouse whistle, this strict scholarly sentinel stalks tirelessly up and down the halls with their trusty sidekick, Deborah the Safety Dragon, in search of any student bold or foolish enough to be cutting class.<br>Any hunter who finds themselves wandering the halls of the School of Sorcery is advised to enroll in a class as soon as possible or else they will suffer the shrill sound of the silver whistle, Deborah's foamy, disapproving growl, and worst of all, the Hall Monitor's most powerful and infamous spell: the Recitation of Infinite Rules! |  |  |  |

# 🐭 Hardboiled Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Hardboiled Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual Spring Egg Hunt event.

| Hardboiled Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (Spring Egg Hunt) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 3,200 | Gold: | 125 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | All types | Other Requirements: | See current Spring Egg Hunt location |
| Cheese: | See current Spring Egg Hunt location | Charm: | See current Spring Egg Hunt location |
| Locations: | Spring Egg Hunt | Loot: | See current Spring Egg Hunt location |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Rain.<br>It never seemed to be sunny-side up in Gnawnia. At least, not whenever I was working a case. This one was a doozie but I knew I was close to cracking it.<br>Like everything else in this crazy world, it all came down to a doe. Easy enough on the eyes, I suppose, but when you're talking about eyes, you have to be careful. This doe had crazy eyes, and a crazy plan hatching behind them.<br>Situations like this never go over-easy. See, she'd gotten ahold of the last free Golden Egg in Gnawnia, and wired up this crazy contraption to it.<br>"A scrambler, get it?" she asked, eyes rolling. And then she laughed. That laugh. Like loose nails rattling around in a jar. Or on reflection, loose screws.<br>Her plan as I figured it, was to scramble up all the Eggscavators in the land and throw mice and hunters alike into wild confusion. Her motivation, however, was about as clear as runny yolk.<br>I had tracked her through Gnawnia's back alleys, from Cheese Bars to Mad Scientist Surplus Shoppes, and finally cornered her here. Still, she didn't seem upset. More pleased, actually. Perhaps at the opportunity to show off.<br>"I'm going to Scramble everything, do you understand? Everything! Ah ha ha ha ha!" That laugh again.<br>"Look, sister," I said, stepping carefully closer. "I know you think you have a job to do, but I do too. I'm here to bring you in, and you can come peacefully with me, or well, I know there's a few hunters around looking for you too, and you're gonna get poached if you're not careful."<br>At the mention of hunters, she flinched visibly. Her shell was crumbling, but breaking her so close to the frying pan was risky. I decided to take it easier on her. "If you hand over the device and come along quietly, we can put you in the Hunter Protection Program. You'll get a new name, a new house, a new job..."<br>"No!" she cried. "No new job! Science, mad science forever!" Then she pulled another strange-looking device from inside her tinfoil jacket, thumbed a button on top, and threw it at me. I ducked, there was a bright flash, and when I looked again, she was gone.<br>As my vision recovered, I noticed a trail of oil, nuts, and bolts leading away. My keen detective sense told me she'd gone that way, so I decided to follow.<br>She couldn't escape me for long. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 465 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the Hardboiled Mouse can be found on the current Spring Egg Hunt event page.

## History and Trivia

- 25 March 2013: The Hardboiled Mouse was released as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2013 event.
- The Hardboiled Mouse's appearance and description are reflective of the hardboiled crime fiction literary genre.

The Hardworking Hauler Mouse have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

Law type traps are very effective against the Hardworking Hauler Mouse. Arcane and Shadow power types are less effective.
The Hardworking Hauler Mouse can only be encountered during the daytime phase in Fort Rox.

- 3 November 2016: The Hardworking Hauler Mouse was release as a part of Fort Rox update.

## History and Trivia

- 14 April 2011: The Hare Razer Mouse was released as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 celebration.
- The Hare Razer Mouse's name is a play on the phrase "Hair Raiser".
- The Hare Razer Mouse's description has changed twice since its release.

> Original version
While he's known as Hare Razer to his minions, he likes to refer to himself as Mr. Fluffy. This is likely to be one of the most insane mice you'll ever meet (most figure it's due to his diet consisting almost entirely of chemically treated candies), however during his rare bouts of lucidity, he manages to run a very effective cartel, smuggling prized hunter items out in tiny delicious but near impenetrable chocolate eggs. If you manage to acquire his mallet the "Rocket Rabbit" you might just have the key to unlocking these eggs.
Spring Egg Hunt 2012 version
While he's known as Hare Razer to his minions, he likes to refer to himself as Mr. Fluffy. This is likely to be one of the most insane mice you'll ever meet (most figure it's due to his diet consisting almost entirely of chemically treated candies), however during his rare bouts of lucidity, he manages to run a very effective cartel, smuggling prized hunter items out in tiny delicious but near impenetrable chocolate eggs.
