# 🐭 Heart of the Meteor

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Heart of the Meteor is a breed of mouse found in Fort Rox.

| Heart of the Meteor |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Meteorite Miners (Dawn Destroyer) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 500,000 | Gold: | 1,000,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | ArcaneShadow | Other Requirements: | Heart of the Meteor phase |
| Cheese: | Sunrise Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Fort Rox | Loot: | Ancient RelicAnimatronic BirdDivine OrbExtreme Ancient CharmFlawless OrbHowliteMeteoric Core FragmentsMeteorite PieceMoon CheeseSimple OrbSilver BoltSuper Ancient CharmSuper Cactus CharmUltimate CharmUltimate Ancient CharmUltimate Attraction CharmUltimate Luck CharmUltimate Power CharmUnstable Charm |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Nestled within the remnants of the Fort Rox meteor slumbers an incredible source of power. This tiny mouse courses with energy, presumed to be what radiates throughout the Fort Rox mice, causing all of the unique transformations.<br>Seemingly at peace and unaware of her surroundings, she is in a deep and powerful trance, emitting waves of bright, blinding light to supply her followers with their great strength and abilities.<br>Nothing can break her trance, save for the alluring source of sunlight from Sunrise Cheese... |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 857 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Heart of the Meteor is only attracted to Sunrise Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

To attract the Heart of the Meteor, the Hunter has to arm Sunrise Cheese in Heart of the Meteor phase in Fort Rox. A Fort Rox Portal Projector is required to enter this phase. Doing so will consume the Projector and add a Ruined Portal Projector to the Hunter's inventory.

The Heart of the Meteor is primarily weak to Arcane traps, though Shadow traps can be used to lesser effect.
The Hunter's Fort defenses have no effect in this phase. Tower Mana cannot be armed while hunting in this phase.
The Heart of the Meteor's power will be lowered with each attraction, to the point where it is a guaranteed catch on the 10th attraction.

Catching the Heart of the Meteor will return the Hunter to the Dawn phase and disarms Sunrise Cheese.

Upon catching the Heart of the Meteor, the following message is displayed in the Hunter's Journal:

> My trap somehow subdued the ferocity of the Heart of the Meteor!
With the creature defeated, the power of the Fort Rox Portal Projector faded and I found myself transported back into the Dawn, almost as if no time had passed at all.
I disarmed my Sunrise Cheese, as it would be no use here.
I must craft another Fort Rox Portal Projector and venture in for another challenge!

The Hunter may enter the Heart of the Meteor phase multiple times without affecting their Wall health or progress in the Dawn phase.

## History and Trivia

- 15 March 2017: The Heart of the Meteor was released with the expansion of the Fort Rox area.

As of 18 November 2015, the High Roller Mouse does not consume cheese or charms when attracted.

All power types are of normal effectiveness against High Roller Mouse with exception of Parental which is ineffective.
The current details regarding hunting strategy can be found here.

| Light and nimble, the Hookshot Mouse is the latest and greatest in raider engineering. Fool's Gold fused with iron has created a strong, light-weight alloy. Coupled with a steam-and-crank power source, this guy is one raider who just won't quit!<br>Harnessing that power allows the Hookshot Mouse to fire his trademarked Hook-Paw launcher from nearly one thousand feet away, allowing them to quickly mount the train and start the assault before they can even be seen!<br>Many a train passenger fears the clanking of little hooks as these mice begin their attack. That definitely isn't rain you're hearing! |  |  |  |

Hookshot mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against the Hookshot Mouse. While an Automice encounter is guaranteed during a Trap Check, it is also possible to attract this mouse during normal hunting.

# 🐭 Hope Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Hope Mouse is a rare breed of mouse found in the Bazaar and Training Grounds.
The Hope Mouse used to be released on several occasions in multiple locations to increase awareness about disasters around the world.

| Hope Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Followers of Furoma |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 2,000 | Gold: | 2,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | TacticalPhysical | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | BazaarTraining Grounds | Loot: | Cheese Belt Token PackCheese Claw Token PackCheese Fang Token PackLucky Power CharmSplintered WoodSuper Lucky Power Charm |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| The Hope Mouse is altruistic by nature, wanting only to help others, be they human or mouse. The King has been so touched by the selflessness of these mice that he has sent out a decree. If any hunter should catch a Hope Mouse, dust him off and send him on his way, as he's likely off to do some good in the world.<br>He can be found scrounging for supplies in the Training Grounds and the Bazaar to give to those in need. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 100 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Hope Mouse is attracted to Gilded and Standard Cheese in the Training Grounds, but only Standard Cheese in the Bazaar.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Hope Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | Physical | None |

## Loot

The Hope Mouse can drop:

- Cheese Belt Token Pack
- Cheese Claw Token Pack
- Cheese Fang Token Pack
- Lucky Power Charm
- Splintered Wood
- Super Lucky Power Charm

## History and Trivia

- 14 January 2010: The Hope Mouse was first released as the developers' response to the earthquake in Haiti.
Original description included additional two paragraphs that were later removed:

> Events unfolding in Haiti have sprung this mouse into action. We will be donating five cents to the Mission of Hope in Haiti for the first 200,000 of these mice caught. By sounding your horn you will help us reach our goal of raising $10,000.
We greatly encourage all MouseHunters to find a charity they are comfortable with and get involved.
When it was released, it could be found in various locations throughout the kingdom (Bazaar, Calm Clearing, Cape Clawed, Dojo, Great Gnarled Tree, Harbour, Laboratory, Meadow, Mountain, S.S. Huntington II, Town of Digby, and Training Grounds), but was later confined to just Training Grounds.

- Between 26 August and 21 September 2010: The Hope Mouse was released in various locations again, in response to the flooding in Pakistan. Its description has been changed, with the following paragraph added:

> Recent events in Pakistan have sprung this mouse into action! Many organizations, such as the Red Cross and World Vision, are currently helping out. Please consider giving them your support.
During that time the Hope Mouse could be found in Bazaar, Calm Clearing, Cape Clawed, Dojo, Great Gnarled Tree, Laboratory, Town of Gnawnia, Training Grounds, and S.S. Huntington II.

- 19 January 2011: The Hope Mouse sprang back into action, in response to flooding in several countries; the following paragraph was added to its description:

> Recent flooding in Australia, Brazil, Philippines and Sri Lanka have sprung this mouse into action! Many organizations, such as the Queensland Government, are currently helping out. Please consider giving them your support.

- 11 March 2011: The description changed in response to an earthquake and tsunami in Japan and the coastal areas across the Pacific basin. The following sentence was added:

> Recent natural disasters occurring in Japan and other parts of the world have sprung this mouse into action!
During that time the Hope Mouse could be found in a variety of locations including Calm Clearing, Cape Clawed, Dojo, Mountain, Town of Digby, Town of Gnawnia, Training Grounds, S.S. Huntington II and Windmill.

- 13 November 2013: After Typhoon Yolanda hit the Philippines, a note was temporarily added to its description:

> Join the discussionto see how you can help out too!

- 19 March 2024: The Hope Mouse was updated as part of the Valentine's Day event retirement. It's description was also updated to include the sentence:

> He can be found scrounging for supplies in the Training Grounds and the Bazaar to give to those in need.
Points: 1,000 -> 2,000
Gold: 1,000 -> 2,000

## External Link

- Mission of Hope

| When it comes to eating cheese off a trap, three heads are better than one! Quick and agile, the three headed Hydra Mouse furiously attacks a hunter's trap in search of a morsel of cheese. Their regeneration abilities make them especially hard to catch. This mouse was designed by MouseHunt fan Neilson Cheng! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 101 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

- Lagoon

> The Hydra Mouse has been found to be especially attracted to Gnarled and Wicked Gnarly cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

- S.S. Huntington IV

> The Hydra Mouse can be attracted by standard cheese, but it has a distinct preference for SUPER|brie+.

## Hunting Strategy

Hydro traps are very effective against the Hydra Mouse. Physical and Tactical traps are of normal effectiveness, but could perform better than a weak Hydro trap.

The Hydra Mouse drops the Ship Blueprints in the Lagoon and Bolt of Cloth, Rope, Scrap Metal, Simple Orb, and Splintered Wood in both locations.

## History and Trivia

- Unknown Date: The Hydra Mouse was designed by MouseHunt fan Neilson Cheng!
- 4 June 2009: The Hydra Mouse is released in the Lagoon.
- 25 January 2011: The Hydra Mouse began dropping Simple Orb as loot.
- 14 April 2011: The Hydra Mouse began dropping a bonus Hydra Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 29 July 2015: The strength of the Hydra Mouse was decreased. The quantity of Bolt of Cloth, Rope, Scrap Metal and Splintered Wood dropped by Hydra Mouse was increased.

Hydrologist Mouse is only available when Docked in Sunken City.

# 🐭 Hydrophobe Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Hydrophobe Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Rodentia region. It can be attracted and caught only during the Spring Season. They are not known to drop any loot.

| Locations: | Seasonal Garden | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Most mice living in Zugzwang's garden have some desire to fit into their seasonal area, but the Hydrophobe mouse proves a rare exception to the rule. To a Hydrophobe, wet fur is a misery, and so they seek shelter under anything they can find (leaves, roots, a looming Deathbot) in order to dodge mother nature's attacks on their well-being. Their dislike for spring rain is so strong, in fact, that it counteracts the seasonal magic. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 250 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Hydrophobe Mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Strong Physical weapons are recommended in order to catch this mouse. Alternatively, hunters may equip their best Tactical trap.

## History and Trivia

- Hydrophobe Mice were introduced on 17 August 2010 with the release of the Seasonal Garden location.

## Preferred Cheese

The Hypnotized Gunslinger Mouse have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Arcane traps are very effective against the Hypnotized Gunslinger Mouse. Shadow traps are of normal effectiveness.
The Hypnotized Gunslinger Mouse can only be encountered during the Midnight, Pitch, Utter Darkness, and First Light phases at Nighttime in Fort Rox.

## History and Trivia

- 3 November 2016: The Hypnotized Gunslinger Mouse was release as a part of Fort Rox update.
